Current docs (`https://pg-odyssey.tech/configuration/rules.html`) require `database { user { authentication, storage, pool, pool_size ... } }` plus `storage` blocks and explicit `authentication`. Config rewrite needed, doc links in `poolduel/docs/configs/odyssey.md`.
